- **rfdiffusion3**: Install, validate, teach, and run RosettaCommons Foundry/RFdiffusion3 workflows on a local or cluster Python/GPU environment. Use when the user mentions RFdiffusion3, RFD3, Foundry, rc-foundry, RF3, ProteinMPNN, LigandMPNN, checkpoint downloads, official RFD3 tutorials, RFD3 design JSON/YAML inputs, protein binders, nucleic-acid binders, small-molecule binders, enzyme scaffolds, partial diffusion, or asks to package/run/debug RFD3 jobs. ([makabaka007x/cadd-skill](https://github.com/makabaka007x/cadd-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/rfdiffusion3) · ★57)
- **rfdiffusion3**: Which backbones can rfdiffusion3 (rfd3) generate: de novo, motif-scaffolded, symmetric or binder-against-a-target? Load before composing any rfdiffusion3 run or writing a contig or hotspot spec. Traps: the chain-break literal is `/0` (the colon form silently designs against half the target); `--length min-max` draws one length per batch; rfd3 renumbers every output chain from 1, so downstream residue numbers inherit an offset. ([jlmoraleshellin/ant-comp](https://github.com/jlmoraleshellin/ant-comp/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/rfdiffusion3) · ★1)
- **rfdiffusion3**: Generate protein backbones with RFdiffusion3 (all-atom diffusion) from a JSON spec — unconditional monomers, binders with hotspots, motif/enzyme scaffolding, symmetric oligomers and partial diffusion. Use for interactive single-device backbone generation; for backbone generation at width, and for the flow-matching alternative, see the rfd4-proteina skill. ([ramanathanlab/loam](https://github.com/ramanathanlab/loam/tree/HEAD/skills/diffusion-models/rfdiffusion3))

([kadel/skills](https://github.com/kadel/skills/tree/HEAD/rhdh-catalog-index))
